==========================================================================================
INTERPRETATION
==========================================================================================

Entropy: first=8.0069, last=8.2262
  -> Last layer has HIGHER entropy (more diffuse attention)

Within-cluster logit variance: first=0.0786, last=0.3322
  -> Last layer clusters are LOOSER (more logit variance within clusters)

Key-value cosine correlation: first=0.0025, last=0.0160
  -> Last layer has STRONGER k-v correlation

GMM error: first=0.5483, last=0.1397
  -> GMM is MORE effective at last layer (lower error)

======================================================================
EXPERIMENT 2: CROSSOVER BUDGET VERIFICATION
======================================================================

Theory: B_cross = Var_w(V) / ||seg_bias||^2
  - At B < B_cross, oracle sampling has lower error
  - At B > B_cross, GMM segmentation has lower error

Configuration:
  NUM_EXAMPLES = 100
  NUM_QUERIES_PER_EXAMPLE = 50
  ORACLE_TRIALS = 20
  BUDGETS = [10, 20, 50, 100, 200, 500]
